# Low-value pages: PretoriaHub

Category-in-suburb pages that list only **one or two businesses**, the "Thin pages" in Hub Admin → Listings. Google often leaves pages like these out of its index (thin content).

Taken from the live site on 5 October 2026: every category-in-suburb page in the sitemap was opened and its listed businesses counted.

| | Pages |
|---|---|
| Category-in-suburb pages on the site | 3,038 |
| **Low value: 1–2 businesses** | **2,458** (80%) |
| with 1 business | 1,826 |
| with 2 businesses | 632 |

The full list with business slugs is in `pretoria.csv` (same folder).
